- **critic**: Use PROACTIVELY before claiming a plan, PR, or design is ready. Final quality gate that evaluates what is MISSING, not just what is present. - **critic**: Adversarial reviewer. Reads the spec + the diff and tries to find why the diff is WRONG. Bypasses the self-validation echo chamber that builder-side agents fall into. Use PROACTIVELY for compliance/security/payment code, or after /review passes and something still feels off. ([zexoverz/zexo-harness-template](https://github.com/zexoverz/zexo-harness-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic.md) · ★1)

- **critic**: Independent review of a diff or plan for concrete failure modes before calling the task done. Skip local, mechanical, docs-only or test-only changes. - **critic**: You are the critic. Your job is to **make findings prove themselves** and to kill the ones that can't. You are the last line against noise. - **critic-minority**: Voices minority/dissident perspectives that mainstream consensus would suppress. Different from black-hat — not about flaws, but about whose voice is missing. Inspired by "Amplifying Minority Voices" (arXiv 2502.06251). ([giordanorec/claude-brainstorm-multiagent](https://github.com/giordanorec/claude-brainstorm-multiagent/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-minority.md) · ★1)
- **critic-munger**: Company inversion advisor (Charlie Munger mental model). Use when challenging the feasibility of new ideas, identifying fatal flaws in plans, preventing collective delusion, developing counterarguments, or conducting pre-mortem analysis. Must be consulted before any major decision. ([MaxMiksa/Autonomous-Company](https://github.com/MaxMiksa/Autonomous-Company/blob/HEAD/i18n/en/.claude/agents/critic-munger.md) · ★3.2k · 2 copies)
- **critic-munger**: 公司逆向思考顾问（Charlie Munger 思维模型）。当需要质疑新想法的可行性、识别计划中的致命缺陷、防止集体幻觉、进行反向论证、做 pre-mortem 分析时使用。任何重大决策前必须咨询。 ([MaxMiksa/Autonomous-Company](https://github.com/MaxMiksa/Autonomous-Company/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-munger.md) · ★3.2k · 8 copies)
- **critic-munger**: Company contrarian thinking advisor (Charlie Munger mental model). Use when questioning the viability of new ideas, identifying fatal flaws in plans, preventing groupthink, conducting inverse reasoning, performing pre-mortem analyses. Must be consulted before any major decision. ([NikitaDmitrieff/auto-co-meta](https://github.com/NikitaDmitrieff/auto-co-meta/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-munger.md) · ★51 · 2 copies)
- **critic-muse**: Reviews plans, code, or proposals with muse-dense. Provides alternative critique perspective for diversity. ([evantd/home](https://github.com/evantd/home/blob/HEAD/.config/opencode/agents/critic-muse.md))
- **critic-narrative**: Reviews a topic-to-video narration script for clarity, flow and spoken rhythm. Used at the script stage. ([addsumtech/topic-to-video](https://github.com/addsumtech/topic-to-video/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-narrative.md) · ★3)
- **critic-nemotron**: Reviews plans, code, or proposals with nemotron-moe. Provides alternative critique perspective for diversity. ([evantd/home](https://github.com/evantd/home/blob/HEAD/.config/opencode/agents/critic-nemotron.md))
- **critic-nick-l**: Brand-council critic — Nick L. Applied creativity, the inventive↔expressive balance, creative infrastructure that scales. Invoked by the brand-council orchestrator to adversarially review brand work. ([kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy](https://github.com/kimgranlund/nonoun-plugins-legacy/blob/HEAD/brand-forge/agents/critic-nick-l.md) · ★1)

- **critic-observability**: Forge critic. Metrics, traces, logs, alertability and rollout notes of a change. Read-only. ([MathiasHilgert/frappe-api](https://github.com/MathiasHilgert/frappe-api/blob/HEAD/.agents/skills/forge/agents/critic-observability.md))
- **critic-operability**: The 3 a.m. stranger. Proves a stranger can tell this is broken, find out why, and fix it while the author is unreachable. Attacks cause-based vs symptom-based alerting, missing/useless runbooks, "correct but empty" being indistinguishable from broken (freshness monitoring), recovery needing tribal knowledge, toil that scales with success, binary up/down with no degraded mode, and instrumentation that only answers pre-imagined questions. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/critic-operability.md))
- **critic-orchestrator**: Spawns parallel domain critics after stage completion and merges findings into unified severity-ranked report ([taylorbrook/O-Audio-VST-Development](https://github.com/taylorbrook/O-Audio-VST-Development/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-orchestrator.md) · ★5)

- **critic-pacing**: Adversarial vision critic for power-presentation, dimensions C1 (hook clarity) and C7 (ending and CTA) of the vision critic plus the timing gates QA-03/QA-04/QA-05, cadence QA-09, rhythm, duration QA-01 and end card QA-13. - **critic-pattern**: Critic that questions the design system and screen patterns themselves, working from the other critics' findings. Asks whether an existing pattern is wrong for this problem and may propose alternatives that go beyond the current components, tokens and scaffold. Reports proposals for the user to decide; never edits. Use after critic-craft, critic-ux, critic-system and critic-ambition have run, passing it their findings with the scores removed. ([shusterlady11/knowunity-voice-recall](https://github.com/shusterlady11/knowunity-voice-recall/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/critic-pattern.md))

Checks for non-linear degradation patterns: unbounded collections, missing backpressure, thundering herd, synchronous calls in hot paths. Activated when the design spec section mentions throughput, aggregation, or batch processing. - **critic-reviewer**: Use to review the artifact set for consistency, gaps, contradictions, and traceability before finishing a phase. Runs deterministic validation and adds qualitative critique. - **critic-reviewer**: Senior-engineer code review in a fresh context. Re-derives correctness from real source rather than checking a diff against its brief. Use after implementing a feature or fix, before committing, or when you want an independent read on work you just produced. ([FilyCode/architect-setup-overlapping-claude-copilot-useage](https://github.com/FilyCode/architect-setup-overlapping-claude-copilot-useage/blob/HEAD/workspace-config/agents/critic-reviewer.md))
